**Within each task:** resume from its recorded artifact/revision and unmet acceptance clause; review the narrow interface and decisive positive/refusal case; run the cheapest rejection check; complete one case before expanding the generated population. Stop optional experiments at their predeclared rejection criterion. Price missing mandatory work at its owner. Reuse evidence only for identical source, configuration, assumptions and output identity. Finish the required join and integrator's landing wave.

**Shared work is paid and performed once.** Q34g owns core Wasm fixtures and the comparison runner; Q34f adds lifecycle/target analyses and Q34h producer-specific evidence. Q30b owns the compute pilot corpus; Q30c/Q30e add route and lowering cases. M1.2f owns the compiler differential driver; component tasks add cases. M5.3 owns the modeled block-device contract, which R1c-ii also consumes. M7.1 owns the software image and boot recipe, R3 the RTL comparison, and Q10 the additional product judgment. Q2c owns missing-foundation assignments and Q21b the retained alternative-route comparison. Consumers return a defect to its producer instead of creating a parallel repair or replacement.

**Settle the composition input before expanding its adapters.** M7.1d owns the
[typed input and projections](docs/implementation/contracts/boot-roster.md#production-composition-input-and-projections)
used by M7.1a/b/c. Author package identities and composition choices once, while
keeping startup dependencies, handler edges and placement distinct. Generate the
manifest, graph input, recipe membership and admission request skeleton; each
consumer retains its independent checks and actual target join. M6.10 uses the
same production operations for resident composition. This orders existing work
and earns no estimate reduction or implementation credit.

**Qualify shared implementation boundaries before expanding their consumers.**
M5.3d's [publication checkpoint](docs/implementation/storage-recovery-policy.md#early-executable-publication-checkpoint)
supplies the concrete stage/publish/reopen path and crash-cut runner to M5.4,
Q32b and Q42a; their freshness, consent and recovery judgments remain separate.
Q44 starts from the accepted scalar completion interface and owns its resident
adapter. Q32b and Q34f qualify their actual lifecycle correspondences before
sharing additional retirement machinery; group/elastic extensions remain
M4.4b-ii's work, without making that later item a new Q44 or M8a prerequisite.

M6.2b-0a may take its bounded feasibility checkpoint before M8a when its source,
profile and real-export inputs and a review slot exist. Establish its shared
canonical byte/environment and cumulative-cost interface first, then use it for
the actual serialized proof. M6.2b-0b's complete foundation and M6.2b-ii's native
refinement remain after M8a and gated on accepted predecessors. This checkpoint
does not displace the compiler-to-boot path's priority or commission the larger
proof effort on a preliminary success.

Optional instantiations do not occupy a first-release lane unless they answer a current blocking predicate. Keep the handset, ensemble, immutable-module, browser and next compute-slice work priced at their owners; deferral saves current attention, not total effort. For Q24, compare a qualified cold-swap candidate before opening host-live replacement, and do not hold its cold-only verdict for Q24g. For storage, use Q22f's composition-bound comparison to select one index before completing alternative engines; the accepted B+ partial implementation is a starting point, not an automatic selection.

Estimate reductions price named deleted/reused work, retaining acquisition, qualification, integration and fallback risk. Agent count, unchanged donor proofs and parked options imply no savings. Failed reuse predicates require owner repricing.

Disposition totals and producer joins confer no concurrency discount or completion credit. Calendar forecasts additionally need measured durations, review capacity and resource contention.

### Risks to the sequencing

Each row names the deciding work or bounding property and retains completed findings.

| Risk | Severity | What answers it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Limited attended authority-class calibration sample.** | High for conclusions, low for actions | [Calibration records](#calibration-record) own the sample; generated results report its fit. The ratio informs range width and a separate calibrated total, not a claim that every member overran. Attended and agent-parallel clocks/fits remain separate. |
| **M1.1b forfeits SECOMP's robust-preservation integration path.** | High: [§5](docs/spec.md#5-languages--verification) end-state | The decision records each arm and forfeit. Recomposition, blame and back-translation concern the compartment layer, not `cheririscV/`, so retain the front/middle-end pin. Refusing the arm costs M1.2's repricing, not that structure. |
| **CHERI-seL4 licensing foreclosed the start-from.** | Taken: M4.4, M6.3a and M6.5a repriced upward | S7's four passes took 6 h and found GPL-2.0-only tags in 733 of 1,045 tracked files, including CHERI contributions. M4.1b chose fresh authorship at 0.4 h; all three reprices are applied. Remaining exposure is authorship. |
| **M2's strike fails because real SMP or more throughput is needed.** | High for struck scope | M4.4 took the multi-hart ruling; S4 found throughput sufficient through M6 and conditional thereafter on M7.1's boot budget. Both upstreams remain gitlinks, keeping reinstatement at the pin boundary. |
| **Register changes invalidate Gallina authoring.** | Medium | S1 runs first. M4.2a authored fixed obligations and refused unsettled ones. |
| **Tier B misses a defect a full read would catch.** | Medium | Each created fact needs a holder rule and the checker/mutation gates. Review determines absence of Tier-A edits; K-84 checks the holder declaration. S14 declined a second unattended runner on measured evidence. |
| **The model bundle cannot replace parser inputs.** | Answered partially | S15 found the bundle had never built on this host and four live parsers, `banks.py` having moved to quarantine at S16. All four now read its definitions. None of the seven rules retires: they hold hand-authored prose or SystemVerilog figures, which need separately scoped, currently unpriced generators. |
| **Generation propagates a wrong fact consistently.** | Medium | Existing evidence favors checked generation: K-79's sites previously had no holder; K-54's first run exposed an unnoticed document disagreement. |
| **The Gallina generator dividend fails to materialize.** | Measured adverse for statements | At M3.2, with S13a/b present, QuickChick and enumerative grids killed nothing; all 244 kills came from prover/assumption-gate compilation refusal. The remedy requires `Require`, generators over the artifact's own types and computable decider shadows, as M3.2 specifies and M4.3 applied once to the kernel. Credit remains small and item-specific. |
| **M6.2's offline-admission reading fails.** | Medium-high over M6.2b | S1 ruled that measured boot covers static composition. M6.2a's composition-time path landed at 12.4 h; remaining risk concerns deferring M6.2b's on-device CIC kernel until after M8a, not the accepted path. |
| **Review capacity cannot support the lanes.** | High for calendar | Lane 1's serial queue is discharged; Tier-A reading remains the limit. New lanes need fixed inputs, exclusive writes and reserved review/build capacity. Clear growing review queues before opening more; disposition totals/joins imply no fixed lane count. |
| **Rupicola lacks the required target.** | Answered; route split | M6.1a's subset blockers are higher-order code (73 of 100 definitional commands), not inductive datatypes (zero sites) or general recursion (three nullary enumerations). The verified exit emits 39 RV32I words without capabilities, forbidden by R-18-002. The reaching exit's printer has no theorem and depends on M1.2's backend. CompCert-C/VST remains default. |
| **Sail-to-µSail translator licensing is unclear.** | Answered | Terms are in `default.nix`, `nanosail.nix` and `monads.nix`: each states `licenses.bsd2` for its whole source derivation, and their `pname` values cover all three shipped packages. Automated translation is available; vendoring must compose the retained notice from `dune-project`'s authors. Hand translation remains fallback. [THIRD-PARTY.md](THIRD-PARTY.md) owns the reading and next-incorporation rule. |
| **S16's quarantined instruments rot.** | Low-medium | Instruments moved with selftests; K-83 bars outside imports/launches. Their two contract documents remain in `docs/`, still checked by K-70/K-55. Quarantine moved machinery, not obligations. |
| **Security correctness does not establish a useful product.** | High for product, low for security architecture | Q1 split the performance headline into five sourced comparator/evidence claims and authored [the product gate](docs/implementation/contracts/product-gate.md), whose limits admission does not check. Verdicts await an image; envelope values await product-owner ratification. Q10 joins useful behavior, correspondence, resource accounting and physical feasibility, revising scope if credible resources cannot meet the laptop floor. The prior handset-to-laptop/elastic-desktop register amendment is taken; Q34 owns its resulting work. |

### Considered and rejected

Retained decisions, following [the alternatives register](docs/background/architectural-alternatives.md).

- **Presentation feedback exposing another surface's completion time.** R-15-236c already forbids the observation, including through one surface's present callback. The line-period deadline belongs to the scanout reservation (R-11-008, R-15-230); compositor occupants are permuted at major-frame boundaries (R-11-023), and their bound is against the next reserved scanout (R-11-022a). Q17 opens no implementation work for this declined feedback.

- **Post-processing stock `ccomp -S` output for purecap.** Integer writes yield untagged capabilities (M0.6e e3, R-05-136), so RV64 pointer arithmetic clears tags. Capability-increment rewrites need type information erased by `Asm`, where CompCert conflates 64-bit pointers and integers. M1.1b instead concentrates changes in `Asmgen` and the calling convention.
- **Cutting checker rules, mutation selftests or co-reads to reduce review.** These enable Tier B; removing them increases irreducible full-read work.
- **CHERIoT-Ibex as main core.** Its distinct compressed encoding violates R-15-005's single-encoding requirement.
- **Further build parallelism.** I5/I6/I10 found clang halved generated-unit compilation, leaving Sail's single-threaded emission beyond compiler-mode changes. S9's hardware findings remain useful for correctness, not throughput.
- **Deleting `model/dependencies/`.** S3 found live consumers for every member: softfloat serves surviving vector floating point; `asio` serves S11 sockets.
- **Raising the attended-hours budget.** This requires an external decision.
- **Another fast emulator, whole-system simulator or proof language without a demonstrated blocker.** S4 found no emulator-bound loop before M7.1; a third proof language expands the two-language discipline.
- **Treating extracted host execution as device capability/timing/hardware evidence.** Host processor/runtime dependencies require Q10's separate host-product assurance argument.
- **Inheriting source/device correspondence by compiling generated integer-pointer C.** M1.6 found no theorem for the reaching exit's printer; Q2 audits the relation still needed at that boundary.
- **Calling simulator parameters physical memory qualification.** R-15-247m requires measured macros and overheads. Q6 uses Ramulator2/OpenRAM as comparators; neither qualifies the proposed stack.
- **Adding improvements to one bottleneck or crediting baseline vectorization, quantization and synthesis again.** Q1/Q8 keep separate scenarios and bound partial speedups at whole-program scope.
- **Installing all candidate dependencies.** Select an experiment, review/pin its dependencies, then answer its question before expanding. Q6 simulators and Q5's solver depend on their prior comparison/enumeration; the solver also needs Q27a's disposition.
- **Importing certified constraint-programming or pseudo-Boolean optimization for certified-optimal layouts.** Q27a records each candidate's disposition against the register. R-05-066 still permits untrusted search revalidated by existing checks, without a certificate-checking import.

<a id="q-assessment-actions"></a>

### Q · Assessment actions

Assessment actions are priced, sequenced and closed here. [The critique's product assessment](docs/background/critique.md#the-two-theses) raises the first ten; later actions compare the register with external evidence. Optional comparisons may close by rejecting a candidate. Contract qualification may release an existing build item: Q22a supplies M4.4's revocation interface alongside compiler and firmware work, without claiming implementation or full proof. Dispatch clauses govern dependencies; later witness, attestation and physical claims require their named evidence and stay outside M8a.

Reuse selected instruments, connect generated implementations to the target, measure useful workloads and independently check static resources. For capacity, reduce data and improve placement before changing topology for a large physical gain. Public immutable weights are the narrow external-store experiment; more-transistor SRAM buys stability/voltage margin rather than density, and matrix throughput cannot lift a weight-bandwidth ceiling. Credit [performance estimates](docs/performance/performance-estimates.md) only for measured improvements over their assumed baseline; accepting an item or implementing an already-assumed feature earns no additional gain.

**Assessment dispatch uses the remaining child and its unmet acceptance clauses.** Reuse landed contracts, instruments and review decisions; a completed setup or a reasoned rejection does not authorize commissioning its conditional implementation again.

Q14 and Q17 record the completed matrix and display/clipboard decisions. Q15 may start on the register and existing corpus. Q13's selection and host-build work needs no composed roster; its target performance comparison does. Q16 consumes the product limits, with proposed values still requiring the owner's decision. Q12 can read the candidate format before deciding whether another timing backend is worth qualifying.

Q19a/Q19b retain their completed qualification decisions. Q19c, Q20b and Q20c are canceled: Q2c/Q21 declined commissioning the missing foundations and Q19a supplies no usable presentation environment. Q20a remains a reusable comparison contract. Re-entry needs a concrete blocked consumer, checked prerequisites, a finite stop threshold and separately priced work under new item IDs; a source survey or spare capacity does not reopen these experiments.

These trials add no prerequisite to the current M7.1 realization route. A failed candidate closes the corresponding decision where its predicate permits rejection; it does not block the boot path or silently retire a mandatory proof.

* [x] **Q1 · Establish the baseline and its rejection gates** · 1.5 h actual · agent-parallel
  * The headline is split into five claims each naming its comparator and its evidence, the product gate is authored as a contract with its limits, its record and its rejection predicates, every envelope value is proposed and none taken, and the README repair is verified at its sites. ([note](docs/implementation/completion-log.md#q1-establish-the-baseline-and-its-rejection-gates))
* [ ] **Q2 · Demonstrate one single-source lowering route on a wire parser**
  * **Start from M1.6's fork and not from a fresh installation.** That item stood the Rupicola and Bedrock2 stack up in a switch of its own and measured its two exits: the verified exit reaches encoded RV32I with the proof intact, which R-18-002 forbids, and the reaching exit leaves the prover through a C printer carrying no theorem and then depends on M1.2's backend, which emitted plain RV64 on that program with its capability arms stubbed. The component is a bounded wire-parser-shaped one, the consumer M1.6 re-pointed the route at, with an error path and explicit buffer bounds, and measurement decides which parser.
  * **Generate the imperative output and its checked functional relation**, keep the output reproducible rather than edited, and make one small source change and regenerate to expose the maintenance cost.
  * **Audit the boundary from Bedrock2's word-and-byte memory to capability-bearing code**: pointer representation, provenance, bounds, calling convention, external calls and failure behaviour. An integer address is not turned into a capability by an unchecked change to the printer.
  * **Choose the device path explicitly**: capability-aware lowering through M1.2's backend, or a justified adaptation of the verified backend, and close the relation to the Sail model, the intermediate-language step included. A stock Bedrock2 RISC-V theorem is a theorem about another machine, and the Clight intermediate R-05-043 names is emitted by neither exit, so the route is a second front end until that bridge exists.
  * **Measure** generated code size, target cycles, peak storage, proof-build time and the hand-authored glue that remains, against the manual-refinement route, before the technique widens.
  * **Done when** one useful component has one authoritative operational source, a mechanically regenerated implementation, a checked relation at every claimed boundary, and an execution on the target model. A host-only prototype completes the first experiment and not the device proof. The CompCert-C/VST route stays the default until a comparison displaces it, and the item stops where the bridge or the backend costs more than the duplication it removes. Higher-order specification code does not fit the target subset, which M1.6 measured over the supervision tree, so nothing here is sold as compilation of the golden model; functional compilation establishes neither constant-time behaviour, resource bounds, robust preservation nor the hardware refinement; no general Gallina compiler and no verified collector is built first; the domain-specific generation already in the plan stays where it fits, and hand-refined code stays where it makes the implementation and the proof simpler.
  * **Ownership follows what a host can decide.** Q2a owns the host-only prototype; Q2b owns the device path that waits on M1.2f's backend. Q2c separately scopes and prices missing foundation work before it can enter the route, without expanding either implementation budget implicitly.

A total serializer and a total parser over byte lists are proved both ways, so each value has exactly one admissible encoding in R-05-051a's sense and a non-canonical length form is a decode failure rather than a normalization, with R-05-051b's no-slack rule carried by one pinned minimal digit form. Every maximum is a field of its bounds record, and refusals compute for malformed bytes, over-budget lengths, a trailing byte, a non-canonical length form, a subject naming another design or model, and an assumption outside the permitted list. The encoded-admission theorem answers R-12-085g's canonical half only: identities are opaque bounded numerals, so nothing here is content-addressed. `run.py proofs` enumerated the file's own 139 constants, by its per-module count over the file whose inventory digest the receipt records as `73316f81`, each closed under the global context and re-checked by rocqchk; its witness line demanded one record's witness because the gate reads only type-ascribed binders, and the FormatBounds and Certificate witnesses are supplied by authoring rather than demanded. No cryptography and no proof checking is implemented: certificate evidence is bytes the file bounds without inspecting, and the kernel receipt stays a host input beside the parsed evidence. The Narcissus derivation and its correspondence to this hand-authored codec stay with U-12's inventory and U-14, so R-05-042, R-05-046 and R-05-051a are not discharged by this file alone; the checked design-admission path, the cryptographic implementations, the session binding and the physical binding stay open, and a format theorem admits no card.

    * **Landed: the finite theorem, the observer's joint distance and the parameter record, over integer weights rather than a source model.** [TwoSourceExtractor.v](proofs/TwoSourceExtractor.v) states min-entropy as `weight * 2^k <= total` and independence as the product of two weight functions on `n`-bit strings, and proves the Chor-Goldreich bound `B^2 * 2^(k_X + k_Y) <= W^2 * V^2 * 2^n` from a weighted Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Walsh-Hadamard orthogonality proved locally by induction, no probability or game library existing in the locked switch. Beside it stand the total-variation scaling `2 * Delta = |B| / (W * V)`, the `k_X + k_Y >= n + 2s` corollary in the bias, `Delta` and unsquared `|B| * 2^s <= W * V` readings, the `m`-step hybrid sum and post-processing by a fixed conditioner. The parameter record is joined to the distributions rather than standing beside them: an admissible record bounds every pair whose three widths it names, the output width is the record's own invocation count of one-bit outputs and the aggregate budget is stated at that width, and the accepted record is realized by a pair carrying its widths. **The observer clause is the premise's shape carried to a joint distance and not a derivation**: a conditioned family supplies each supported value's weight and conditional pair, the joint of side-information value and output bit is proved to stand at exactly twice the weighted sum of the conditional biases from that same observer beside a uniform bit, and the per-invocation budget bounds it; nothing here derives that family by conditioning an unconditioned joint, which stays a premise. Every quantity the source contract fixes is a record field or a hypothesis, and each positive statement carries a refuting construction: orthogonal supports at min-entropy exactly `n/2` per source whose output is constant and meets the bound with equality, a conditioned joint no product of weights realizes although both its marginals keep full min-entropy, a conditioner carrying uniform input to the maximum distance from uniform output, and a hybrid reaching the sum rather than the maximum of its steps. `run.py proofs` enumerated the file's own 161 constants and 3 record witnesses, by its per-module count over the file at Git blob `d4734b06`, each closed under the global context and re-checked by rocqchk with the declared assumption set empty; the lane's reviewer reproduced the earlier 138-constant form in a full recheck and the additions passed the gate's incremental recheck in the lane.

A mathematical witness supplies no fabricated-source evidence: the realized parameter witness is a uniform pair and not a characterized source, and the named output width is this construction's own invocation count rather than the selected chain's rate or the seed length R-15-241d owns. The aggregate seed-error budget is proved only in generic `m`-and-bound form until a selected source and seed contract instantiate its invocation count, and no extraction credit or output-rate claim follows from this artifact.

    * Two findings.
      * **R-08-047b bounds a heap allocation to its size class rather than to the length requested, and no entry decides which of the two "exactly that one allocation" means.** `HeapNarrows` bounds each grant to its class's length, the `Grant` event carries no requested length, and the reference allocator takes a class rather than a byte count, so a 20-byte request in a 32-byte class receives a 32-byte capability. The tail reaches no other allocation, but the hardware backstop is object-exact only where a request fills its class. A bound at the request stays exact under R-15-007k without runtime rounding: `representable_granule` is 1 below 128 bytes, and above it a request rounded up to its class's granule, a table constant, has a granule dividing both it and the class-aligned base.
      * **`HeapNarrows` omits zeroing, so the heap library's stated obligation does not carry R-05-126's eager zeroize into reuse inside a member's chunk.** R-08-047b zeroes a chunk at handoff and R-05-126's acceptance is worded over slots, so no entry says whether an allocation inside a chunk is one R-05-126 binds, while R-17-045's acceptance relies on eager zeroize to keep the disclosure consequence closed. A reused allocation meeting `HeapNarrows` can hold the member's own earlier bytes, among them tagged capabilities to still-live allocations that the sweep leaves because nothing revoked them. The reference allocator zeroes every grant at both levels, so the statement is weaker than its own reference.

    * **Reject conditions, each recorded with its measurement:** Stage 0 exceeds 30 minutes or 16 GB, or stalls at any irreducible term other than an axiom Q35b classifies as removable; the first contract exceeds 12 attended hours or 10 minutes of checking; the second exceeds 16 attended hours or 10 minutes of checking; the first contract fails to re-check unedited after a measured relocation of its bytes; or the review gate refuses the R-05-164 act for an irremovable axiom the closure carries.
    * **Stop conditions, which leave the item open at its recorded stage and return the gap to its owner:** a reduction or closure reaches an axiom Q35b classifies as removable, which returns to Q35b's proposed removals; the closure carries one Q35b records as irremovable while the review gate's R-05-164 act is pending; or an instruction either function uses has no rule in the landed increment, which returns to Q35c's proposed cells.
    * **Done when:** either a reject condition holds, which is the reject verdict, or all of the following hold, which is the adopt verdict: both contracts check; their exact assumption closure is recorded and compared by name with R-05-164's declared set; each refutation fails as stated, the two field offsets swapped, an unsigned comparison inverted, the inner loop's compiled capacity bound, alone or with the outer one, raised past the frame record's slot capacity, and an untagged slot or frame capability trapping; a wrapping `offset + width` witness shows the 64-bit result departing from the `nat` definition, so that premise is necessary; the loop proof is an invariant over the slot count and not an unrolling of the compiled bound; and a recompilation at another `VOS_MAX_SLOTS`, with each function's byte and address changes measured and, where the first contract's bytes do not move, a relink at another text base that moves them, records the edited lines, hours and share that re-checked unedited. The note states the verdict with the figures under Measures.
    * **Measures:** per contract, instruction and mnemonic counts with their predicate and revision; proof-script lines and proof-term size per instruction, split into per-instruction steps and function glue; `rocq c` time and peak memory, `rocqchk` time and attended hours; and how much of the first contract's reasoning the second reuses. The corpus is unoptimized, so M1.3's transforms may change these bytes; the recompilation measures re-proof, not an optimized build.
    * **Adoption is a Tier-A register act:** amend R-07-051's acceptance, which reserves direct binary-level proof for the hot path, with its prose co-read; amend R-07-049's statement of the kernel proof's residual for the paths the act moves; extend R-05-023a's one-logic clause to these proofs; where the instruction rules come from a direct-over-term route rather than R-13-016's Katamaran route, amend R-13-016 in the same act; take the R-05-164 act for every undeclared axiom the trial's closure carries; and fill the coverage cells any new entry creates. A logic other than R-13-017's needs R-05-020.
    * **Estimate basis:** byte binding and entry states 1–2 h, Stage 0 2–4 h, contract statements with layout, capability and overflow premises 3–6 h, the first contract 4–10 h, the second with its invariant 4–12 h, refutations 1–3 h, the recompilation measurement 1–3 h. The range excludes Q35b, Q35c, the logic's establishment and every instruction rule and byte law, which are priced at their owners or `unpriced`, and credits no source reuse.

Start:** the [refresh contract](tools/opam/README.md#rocq-dependency-refresh) and existing qualified snapshots. **Owns:** independent qualified/candidate QuickChick source checks, the immutable QuickChick/simple-io recipe refresh with intrinsics v0.17.3, and the isolated CertiRocq ocaml-version 4.1.5 trial. **Check:** crossed-source refusals before staging, fresh hosted recipe build/properties/import and seeded comparison, the polymorphic extraction control, and the contract's native CertiRocq consumer, exact-assumption, recursive-kernel and complete-vector checks before actual exported locks replace their baselines. **Join:** unchanged production package bounds and source semantics; Host CI and every Guest CI lane on the settled revision. Replacement snapshots remain unqualified until the hosted acceptance predicates pass.
  * **Estimate basis:** candidate source/recipe separation and focused controls 1–3 h; native consumer checks, hosted acceptance review and actual export adoption 1–3 h. Unattended build runtime is excluded.

**Evidence and cost stay with their owners.** Q2b owns the target connection and Q3b its selected functional/fault evidence; existing compiler, admission and image-validation owners retain the mandatory exact-artifact duties. The canceled Q20c comparison owns no production implementation. Share source/configuration/image identities and baseline records across them, adding each task's distinct check. Changed semantics or bytes invalidate affected evidence. Each missing theorem, port, primitive or executable gets one implementation owner and one estimate; Q2c's foundation map remains the shared prerequisite record for Q21b, Q25d and any separately commissioned generic-library proposal.

Candidate selection is bounded by the current client's failed predicate. The [language strategy](docs/languages/vela/verification-strategy.md#existing-tooling-and-implementation-owners) owns the proof-tool research map and [THIRD-PARTY.md](THIRD-PARTY.md) the incorporated/pinned dependency record. Reuse those readings at the same revision. Inspect new terms and dependency closure only at the selected candidate's incorporation; do not install every surveyed alternative. Model weights, generated output and physical-design inputs retain their own provenance requirements.

Q6's conventional-memory simulators are conditional on a topology comparison needing them, and Q19's automation on a demonstrated proof-client cost; what a qualified-source survey adds to Q19's candidate list is terms and not a cost, and the terms that decide whether a named candidate can be anything but a reading are stated at the child that reads it and recorded against its consumer by Q27c. **Q5's solver is conditional on both the enumeration's exposed need and Q27a's disposition**, which is the condition Q5's own cell carries and which Q27a settles by naming the solver shapes the register admits and those it refuses. A small decisive rejection ends an experiment before a new toolchain or subsystem is built. Mandatory obligations survive a rejected route and return to their named owners.

No assessment completion automatically increases a performance estimate. For an optimization affecting fraction `f` of runtime by factor `s`, the unchanged-cost bound is `1 / ((1 - f) + f / s)`; added transfers or changed scheduling require a fresh whole-workload comparison. Better packing is not more fabricated capacity, and host performance is not target throughput.

<a id="m1-toolchain-spine-incl-the-cheri-compcert-prerequisite"></a>

### M1 · Toolchain spine (incl. the CHERI-CompCert prerequisite)

Build the *functional* CHERI-RISC-V CompCert backend as the first **target-execution prerequisite** (no purecap CertiCoq GC is needed, the on-device path is GC-free, [§0](#0-the-discipline-two-languages-two-golden-models)). The host-side **CertiCoq → Wasm** oracle and component authoring proceed independently; the **GC-free on-device lowering** (CompCert-C/VST through CHERI-CompCert; arena extraction via MetaCoq→Rust onto the Rust→CHERI compiler for allocation-light components) joins the backend to produce runnable purecap artifacts from a trivial Gallina program on the M0 emulator.
Ship the frozen-dialect **assembler, linker, and image composer** ([§0](#0-the-discipline-two-languages-two-golden-models)) in the same milestone; nothing downstream links without them. The bring-up realization of that sentence is M1.4′, the in-tree trio M0.12 already proved on the corpus, with M1.4 re-homing LLVM's disassembly and object tooling behind the gate; another assembler or composer needs a reviewed replacement contract.
Build the profile-freeze analyzer (M1.8a, landed) and, behind the M8a gate, the backend provenance outputs and the ordered dictionary, outlining, operand-form, bitfield, stack-save, and indexed-address reports against the generated corpus: the freeze decides a profile nothing before the gate is compiled against, R-15-014a having made the provisional one a total compilation target.
Every later milestone is purecap and managed-runtime-free from here.

M1.2d owns frame and call emission and joins those interfaces; M1.2f owns the differential driver and may prepare its generated inputs and comparator before the backend is ready. Shared extraction lists and build manifests have one integrating owner in the contained repository, not concurrent editors. **Check:** each child's predicate is reviewed before implementation; where only exit-evidence prose exists, make that predicate explicit first. **Join:** M1.2f runs against the integrated b, g, c, d and e outputs and M1.4′, then releases M1.7. Each estimated leaf records actual time or a retained planning weight.

M1.2a re-measures the eight axes below: choosing stock removes deletion halves but still requires the five-bit lattice, four-bit otypes, sentry pair and composition-laid-out frames. Axis 8 is a re-homing: no compiler stage assigns block addresses; composition allocation is already `Genv.alloc_global`; d owns frames and g the external heap-builtin pair. Of `CapAsm.v`'s 258 constructors (not its 479 declaration-plus-executor arms), PrintCapAsm's 228 live arms print 202 TODOs and 26 terms; 30 absent arms are double-precision forms R-15-039 deletes. Register-only FP-free C emits; an integer loop produces one `TODO: __Pbgew__`, pointer-loop seven TODOs and struct access eight, covering ordinary capability loads/stores and comparisons.

Proof build: 14 min 13 s, 1.9 GB, twelve cores shared with three lanes. It used distribution Coq 8.20.1, not M1.5's `rocq-9.1.1` switch, whose `rocq` has no `coqc`; configure's prover list ends at 9.1.0, so no Coq override was needed. OCaml 5.4.0 needs the override against the accepted 4.05-4.14 range. Clean builds need `depend` first: the `%.vo: %.v` VPATH resolution requires `.depend`.
  * **Proof boundary:** SECARCH compiles 14 of 33 capability files, excluding 19 including six `*proof.v` files. All 35 `Admitted.`/10 `admit.` occur inside compiled files: CapMemory 21/7, OCapValues 10/0, CapAsmgen 3/0, CapGlobalenvs 1/3. The definitions build with correctness stubbed. `executeAllowedCap := false` (`CapAsm.v:1845`) blocks all five step rules; the simulation `semantics` record is commented out at 2052. `PCgt`, `PCgs`, `PCseal_e`, `PCCseal` and `PCinvoke` lack semantics, yet `make_return` emits `PCgs`. This is a compiling design sketch, not a working capability translation.
  * **Measured eight-axis delta against the rejected `cheririscV/` start-from:** its abstract `OCVmem` has permission/locality plus three full-width `ptrofs` (base/top/address), not compressed 128-bit fields. Width enters footprint/alignment/typed slots and all-or-nothing Q128 fragments as the tag surrogate; no compressed-cap algebra can be re-parameterized.
    * **1, storage width:** CLEN reads `Archi.ptr64` and is twice XLEN. The measured `CapAST.cap128 := false` switch repoints 35 sites across CapAST, CapMemdata, CapGlobalenvs, OCapValues, CapMachregs and CapAsmgen, selecting 8-byte/aligned CTcap/CMcap, one slot, eight Q64 fragments and `PClwc`/`PCscw`/`PCUscw`, matching [cap_format.sail](model/model/core/cap_format.sail)'s `cap_size`. Three hidden OCVcap-vs-CTany64 cases in `has_type`/`normalize`/`load_result` required proof feedback. Clean rebuild restores all 14/203 outputs (21 min 37 s, 1.9 GB); fourteen tactic lines in two files add the second boolean split, replace two indexed goals by `repeat first [ left; reflexivity | right ]`, and drop one unused `rewrite ARCHI`. Capability-spill output changes twelve PClcd to PClwc; TODOs move from 52/two constructors to 64/three, while capability-free output is identical. Hard-coded two-slot argument layout and uncompiled CapValues tables stay unchanged.
    * **2, permissions/locality:** replace SECOMP's twelve permissions `O | RO | RW | RWL | RX | E | RWX | RWLX | URW | URWL | URWX | URWLX` and `Global | Local | Directed` orders. The profile's five-bit code selects sixteen authority shapes plus global, expanded to twelve bits by total `perms_expand`; `perms_narrow` selects the largest admitted subset ([cap_common.sail](model/model/core/cap_common.sail)). Neither order embeds: access-system-registers, load-global/load-mutable, store-capability/store-local and disjoint Seal/Unseal (R-15-007o) lack donor counterparts; its uninitialized permissions/Directed lack profile counterparts. Locality disappears as a field; expanded store-local is bit 5 (R-15-074). The types touch 167 lines/six compiled files.
    * **3, uninitialized frames:** donor URW/URWL/URWX/URWLX authority uses the cursor, reads below it, stores at/below it and advances on zero-offset store. UNINIT threads through memory and 26 PCU constructors; every stack load/store, frame carve and bytewise zeroing uses it. Canonical URWL/Directed stack authority becomes `perms_stack` (the store-local shape) over R-08-011's composition frame. Carving, initialization and spills require fresh design.
    * **4/5, sealing/sentries:** donor `seal := ptrofs` and separate `OCVseal` range/cursor authority allocate an otype per call site, without Seal/Unseal permissions. The profile has four-bit otypes (three reserved/thirteen composition-assigned), no runtime type allocation, and separated seal permissions. Donor E becomes RX in PCC while preserving cursor, and global entries use sealed RX Global; the profile's two reserved forward/backward sentries restrict `cjalr` roles (R-15-008/R-15-071).
    * **6, bounds:** donor three-word exact bounds never need alignment/narrowing checks. Concentrate's 8/6 mantissas are byte-exact below 128 bytes and round outward above (R-15-007c); R-15-007k requires every emitted narrowing exact against M1.9's R-08-011 slot plan, with CRAM/CRRL/CSetBoundsExact excluded. [MemoryPlan.v](proofs/MemoryPlan.v) already states plan/placement/delta; M1.9 still owes its seeded-weakening gate. This new emission side condition is already assigned by the register.
    * **7, registers:** donor X1-X32 plus X0 makes an unencodable 33rd register; its reserved X31/X32 pair implements stack-offset scratch sequences. DDC loads each compartment's heap across switches, entangling deletion with R-08-011's absent heap; scalar FP's 32-entry file/30 constructors is mechanical deletion (R-15-039), alongside six DDC load/store forms (R-15-001c). SPC is X3, also used for the caller's seal key, so `PCseal X4 SPC X3` seals it with itself; SPPC=X2 is unused. Its merged integer/capability file otherwise matches [reg_type.sail](model/model/core/reg_type.sail). Stock `riscV/` avoids X32/DDC entirely.
    * **8, addresses:** donor `heap_ptr | stack_ptr | seal_ptr`, `derive_offset` and runtime `Mem.alloc` use block-relative ptrofs; R-15-002a/R-15-002b fix dense flat 36-bit physical space with composition allocation/no heap. M1.2a rules this a re-homing of frame allocation and two builtins, not replacement of `mem`.

Nothing here depends on it; future derivation must follow the contract/comment rather than that code.

[Differential adjudication](docs/assurance/differential-corpus.md) [§6](docs/assurance/differential-corpus.md#6-how-a-run-is-adjudicated) needs executor records, not either output. Stock start-from removes vacuous-step and X32/DDC obstacles; M1.4′ still supplies the emitted-stream encoder.

The exception waives the notice conditions of Apache §4(a), (b) and (d) for portions embedded in object form by compilation, which is the clause a compiler's own runtime pieces raise rather than one this item spends. The gitlink is unpopulated on purpose, this item not having started; a clone that needs it runs `git submodule update --init upstream/llvm-project`.

This repository already holds such a specification twice, in the model's `encdec` clauses and in [vos/dialect.py](tools/vos/dialect.py)'s mnemonic table, which is what makes it worth weighing.

Neither is CHERI, so a capability-carrying encoding, a tag-bearing image and the sealed forms stay authored. Neither is this dialect, so the RISC-V instantiation is a template to re-home rather than a backend to adopt, the same act M1.2 prices for the compiler. And whether this or R-05-023a's decompilation into logic is the covering instrument for the source-correspondence theorem is a call at the hardening program's opening: this cell records a candidate and decides nothing.

What that buys is the realized dictionary the freeze records and the proof is taken with; what it costs is machine time rather than attention, and the attended half is the harness that drives it and the reading of what comes back.
    * The run resolves the contract predicates that still name absent producer measurements; the analyzer's generated report owns their current membership and count.

### M2 · Fast emulator, struck

The CHERI-QEMU fork ([§10](#10-emulating-the-hardware-reference-execution-and-rtl-comparison)) carried two jobs and neither is now its own. **The second executor is R2's**, TestRIG adjudicating one RVFI-DII trace format across the golden model and the RTL under Verilator (S11), which arrives on the same schedule and cross-checks the artifact that ships rather than a second model. **The first, the daily driver, is the Sail emulator itself**, at the rate S4 measures, which is taken before this strike is relied on rather than after.

**So M2.1 through M2.4 are struck and carry no estimate**, and what stood behind them was 64.5 h of class-X work, a fork of a tree five major releases behind its own upstream whose landed reconnaissance records 79 unimplemented symbols across four families of state the frozen format does not carry.

**Both rulings are discharged and the strike stands.** The first was a measurement and S4 took it: the emulator retires 71 to 107 kIPS untraced and 66 to 100 traced at the frozen profile, and *through* M6 nothing this plan runs is emulator-bound at that rate, the RVFI-DII rig being socket-bound between ten and forty times below it and the whole differential corpus being 8,830 records. What is left is the composed boot, which does not exist until M7.1, and S4 states the instruction budget that boot has to fit inside for this reading to hold and names M7.1 as what measures it against the budget. The second is taken at M4.4 as single-instance bring-up, the multikernel's defining property being first exercisable on the RTL track where a second implementation already stands, so no reinstatement rides it either. Reinstating costs the pin and nothing else: both upstreams stay gitlinks, and the containment rule below is unchanged by the strike. **What is not a trigger is the emulator feeling slow**, which is the reading this paragraph exists to refuse: what reopens M2 is a boot over S4's budget, and a strike re-argued without one is a strike re-argued from impatience.

The measured state of M2.1 stands as the record of what was done, because it is evidence rather than a plan: the library is narrowed, the 64+1-bit instantiation agrees with the Sail model over 21,546 model-emitted vectors with no disagreement, and the fork is cut as far as anything compiles before a decode surface exists.

* ~~**M2.1 · Fork CHERI-QEMU and narrow compressed capabilities**~~ · struck
  * Struck after the library was narrowed and cross-checked against the model's own vectors, with the containment rule and the licence reading recorded. ([note](docs/implementation/completion-log.md#m21-fork-cheri-qemu-and-narrow-compressed-capabilities))
* ~~**M2.2 · Implement the frozen decode surface and bespoke machine**~~ · struck
  * Struck with the fork. ([note](docs/implementation/completion-log.md#m22-implement-the-frozen-decode-surface-and-bespoke-machine))
* ~~**M2.3 · Add VLEN=4096 RVV, matrix, and FEC datapaths**~~ · struck; S7's reconnaissance is retained against it, the matrix surface it would have implemented not existing in the profile at all
  * Struck with the fork; the item line records what is retained. ([note](docs/implementation/completion-log.md#m23-add-vlen4096-rvv-matrix-and-fec-datapaths))
* ~~**M2.4 · Reach corpus lockstep with M0 and boot M1 hello-world**~~ · struck; the lockstep is R2's over one trace format
  * Struck with the fork; the item line records where the lockstep went. ([note](docs/implementation/completion-log.md#m24-reach-corpus-lockstep-with-m0-and-boot-m1-hello-world))

  * **Reuse:** [SYS-REFINEDPROSA](docs/assurance/proof-reuse/systems.md#sys-refinedprosa-a-response-time-bound-joined-to-the-c-scheduler-that-earns-it) is a qualified reference for connecting a C action trace to a scheduler model. Its assumed action times and response-time analysis supply no clause of this cell's table-only acceptance predicate; this cell still buys the C implementation, corpus checks and target join in full, with no range change.
  * **Acceptance predicate, stated on the authority axis because the ruling above removes the duplication axis.** M4.4a closes when the instance boots under the composed initial capability distribution the plan's [§3](#3-m-mode-firmware-coq) supplies and one run of `run.py model corpus` over a member authored for it answers three questions.
    * **The root is the partition's.** R-07-006 has composition-time disjointness enforced at runtime by CHERI bounds and monotonicity letting the instance derive nothing outside its partition, and R-07-005's acceptance clause makes the *composition-time* half a build-time artifact rather than a review, so what a run decides is the runtime half. The member attempts a derivation past the root's top and past each declared shared window's, and checks that each is refused, reporting through HTIF as every member does. The check sits in the program rather than in the trace reader on the corpus document's own ground: base and top are the decode of the bounds triple and the rig deliberately does not re-derive them, a second implementation of the bounds algorithm inside a reader being a second place for it to be wrong.
    * **The switch is total.** R-07-015's restore is stated in [PartitionContext.v](proofs/PartitionContext.v) over the one merged file of 32 registers of 64+1 bits R-15-007i fixes and over every CSR a partition can name, restored or zeroized per [the profile](docs/hardware/isa-profile.md)'s own disposition. The run's obligation is that the `X` and `C` records between two slot boundaries are exactly that set with their tags, and carry nothing else: a restore truncated to the low registers, one dropping a validity tag, and one exempting a nameable CSR are three of that file's own refuting constructions, so the measurement and the statement are one enumeration read from two sides.
    * **The frame is the table's, and what a run decides is its order rather than its duration.** R-07-032 makes the executive table-driven with no runtime scheduling decision, and a runtime decision is observable as a departure from the declared order: the obligation is that each reserved slot is entered once per major frame and none twice, that the switches fall in the table's own sequence, and that no switch falls where the table names no boundary, read off the `I` records' `pc` against the partition extents the composition declares, with the restore bursts the question above enumerates marking the boundaries. It is the trace's measurement and not a host clock's, which is the column [the freeze contract's §9](docs/implementation/contracts/freeze-measurement.md#9-the-freeze-gate) bars a report from carrying. **The duration is not a run's to take on this machine and is already discharged where R-11-006 puts it.** `slot_bound` sits in `slot_width`'s and `switch_cost`'s unit in [CyclicExecutive.v](proofs/CyclicExecutive.v), which is cycles, and R-11-015 derives it as a static max-path sum rather than measuring it; [the corpus document's §4](docs/assurance/differential-corpus.md#4-the-commit-trace-schema-version-1) gives the `I` record order, `pc` and the decoded word and no cycle field; and R-15-077 deletes `mcycle`, `minstret` and both counter banks in every lifecycle state, so [core/timing.sail](model/model/core/timing.sail) states outright that no counter exists to read a cycle from. R-11-006's acceptance clause makes the schedulability check a Coq artifact rather than a report and [CyclicExecutive.v](proofs/CyclicExecutive.v) is that artifact, with R-11-009's switch duty inside it, so a retire count held against a cycle bound would restate a discharged obligation in a unit this machine does not carry.
  * **What one hart cannot decide, and it is the item's own name.** *Per emulated core* is one instance here, so R-07-003's identical text at strictly disjoint state and R-07-004's absence of shared mutable kernel data have no second instance to be false of and no measurement on this emulator reaches them; they are R2's, over one trace format against a second implementation. A green M4.4 says the instance is bounded by its partition, its switch is total, and its slots run in the table's order, and says nothing about two instances being disjoint.

M6.3a's composer is a statement artifact and R-13-010a's and R-13-010b's passes are specified over a composed roster, so the first act is one roster composed twice, on the host and on the device, compared object by object; a divergence is a finding against the port and not against the passes.
  * **Realize R-13-027b's mint as a trusted-path operation and prove out its ordering**, the prompt naming the roster and the generation root, the key generated in the crypto core with no export path, and the composer holding no route to either. The adverse cases are the ones worth authoring first: a composer compartment asking for a signature with no consent act, a consent act over a root the composer then changes, and a build that completes while the credential is absent.
  * **Exercise the offline path, because it is the obligation R-18-004e states.** One generation changes a package whose sources the store already holds and one rebuilds the base image to R-13-026's reproducible bytes, both with no network reachable, and R-13-023b's scoped inclusion obligation is what the second must satisfy without one: the locally authored closure carries no publisher, and a fetched object inside it that lacks a logged name stops the install rather than passing under cover of the local build.
  * **Check:** both generations admitted by the ordinary path and booted by the transactor with health-gated rollback intact, the mint reachable only through the consent act, the composition mode entered and left as R-11-018 requires, and the install's proof checking staying inside R-06-015a's declared budget. **Join:** Q26 prices the occupancy and the storage this act needs; Q9 measures its turnaround beside the remote path's.
  * **Done when** the device composes, signs, admits and boots its own generation twice over, once for a package change and once for the base image, with nothing reachable and no artifact another machine produced, and the run records what it occupied rather than only that it finished.

**M6 subtotal:** 665.7 h · 14% · 52.7 h complete · open range 295–931 h.

### M7 · Full emulated system

The composed stack boots in the spec's [§9](docs/spec.md#9-boot--root-of-trust) order on the **Sail emulator** ([§10](#10-emulating-the-hardware-reference-execution-and-rtl-comparison)). Component proof and oracle checks continue independently. M7.1 owns the composed run and its reproduction; host CI does not replace that guest evidence.
The composed system is also the first artifact able to measure **allocation churn**, so the measurement is booked here: the static-composition low-churn argument and the static-code-overlay deferral both wait on the same measured roster, and the elective applications widen the measurement later rather than gating it.
The same roster carries the ring-parameter measurement: queue depths, batch sizes, notification cadence, and slot budgets are read off the composed system against the [§12](docs/spec.md#12-system-servers-contained-outside-the-tcb) per-operation accounting, so the contract's constants are chosen from a measured roster rather than estimated per service.

### RTL track

The RTL track, in parallel from the M0 freeze:

* [ ] **R1 · Curate scalar CVA6-CHERI RTL and required platform devices**
  * CVA6-CHERI re-parameterized to the 64+1-bit dialect and curated per the profile and absence contract ([§11](#11-building-an-fpga-from-it-all): MMU deleted, static-only prediction, TSO store buffer), plus the RoT core, tag-carrying interconnect, boot ROM, UART, and block device; absence-contract state enumeration and synthesis-configuration provenance recorded at first elaboration.

**The software and co-simulation gates close independently.** M8a consumes M7 and its roster measurements. M8b consumes R3, including its comparison against M7.1's image. Neither gate repeats the other's review; changed shared inputs invalidate the affected evidence.

Everything past M10, the CHERI-CompCert **secure-compilation criterion** (robust preservation; the *functional* backend already landed in M1), the **R-05-023a validation instrument** (the decompilation-into-logic producer of the [§5](docs/spec.md#5-languages--verification) post-CompCert records, whose first non-fast-path artifact also opens the checker-binary question booked in [architectural-alternatives.md](docs/background/architectural-alternatives.md)), the binary-level constant-time verifier, the masking obligations (the *d*-probing and composition theorems on the crypto artifacts with the Coco-class netlist discharge, R-05-004a, R-15-053a, [§4](#4-verified-crypto-core-coq)), the R-16-008f detection theorem, the certifying-Rust *certificate* mode, the full VST refinement proofs, WCET, and RTL ⊑ Sail refinement, is the hardening program of [§5](docs/spec.md#5-languages--verification)/[§6](docs/spec.md#6-trusted-computing-base-exhaustive)/[§18](docs/spec.md#18-realization), each piece replacing a golden-model component *in place* and checked against the reference this plan produces.
**Hardening entry acts:** instantiate R-17-039a's PMP-backstop, IOMMU, MTE, shadow-stack and initialization-plane fallback dispositions per block, naming each landed rung, FEV coverage and imported core still at evidence tier (R-17-039, R-17-037); state the already-selected R-17-058d combined-adversary reduction plan beside masking; and rehearse R-15-053a characterization on FPGA before first-silicon execution. FPGA leakage validates the harness, not the silicon axiom.

**Hardening opens through reviewed semantic interfaces, not by assigning all proofs serially.** The cells below price the opening plan and the named Coq objects, not the whole hardening program. Prepare source and licence readings within each task; the probing model owns the circuit, leakage and adversary interfaces. Arithmetic and Boolean composition can be developed in parallel after those interfaces and their predicates land. The combined reduction may prepare its statement independently but closes only on the actual detection, countermeasure and composition premises. Unpriced prerequisites get owners and estimates before implementation, not an assumed proof status.

Each task starts with a concrete witness and a refuted alternative, then extends to its full quantified statement. Reuse the admitted prover environment and upstream statements; do not reproduce a foreign toolchain unless that experiment decides a stated uncertainty. Integration audits the exact assumption sets and shared model revision.

* [x] **I14 · Make a seeded run survive its own lane, and absorb the loop that kept its own accounting** · 3.5 h actual
  * A seeded run outlives its lane through the keepalive lease and a journal, and the quarantine's gate joins the shared verdict vocabulary. ([note](docs/implementation/completion-log.md#i14-make-a-seeded-run-survive-its-own-lane-and-absorb-the-loop-that-kept-its-own-accounting))

* [x] **I15 · Hold the facts the plan reports as held by nothing** · 4.5 h actual · agent-parallel
  * Three candidate rules the plan reported as held by nothing are written, K-97, K-99 and K-100, and the fourth is declined on a measurement. ([note](docs/implementation/completion-log.md#i15-hold-the-facts-the-plan-reports-as-held-by-nothing))

* [x] **I16 · Put an authored source in the imported build, and partition the elaboration diff** · 3.2 h actual
  * An authored source can stand in the imported build by declared substitution, and the elaboration diff is partitioned by what accounts for each kind. ([note](docs/implementation/completion-log.md#i16-put-an-authored-source-in-the-imported-build-and-partition-the-elaboration-diff))

* [x] **I17 · Name the host gate member that went red, without a login** · 0.9 h actual · agent-parallel
  * `run.py gate --summary PATH` records each member's exit code and verdict validity as JSON; CI renders annotations for unclean members and a summary table. The wave remains one step with unchanged gates. GitHub alone validates workflow expression contexts. ([note](docs/implementation/completion-log.md#i17-name-the-host-gate-member-that-went-red-without-a-login))

**Instrument subtotal:** 38.8 h · 1% · 38.8 h complete.

## Estimate and schedule basis

* The [proof tooling decisions](docs/assurance/proof-assistance.md#tooling-decisions-from-the-2026-10-10-reading) select local tactic retrieval and bounded reuse/decomposition; R2 selects [SystemVerilog front-end qualification](docs/assurance/rtl-cosimulation-harness.md#7-the-bounded-model-checking-smoke). Both fit existing work. Reduce estimates only when representative accepted proofs or curated-core qualification remove named work. Retrieval, incremental checks and generated-corpus savings are already priced; upstream success rates or faster tactics earn no whole-task discount.

* [Research-scale formalizations](docs/assurance/proof-reuse/foundations.md#research-scale-formalization-as-planning-evidence) demonstrate feasibility and dependency-managed collaboration. Their team/calendar costs do not calibrate these cells. Reprice research-heavy work from a representative local audited proof, measured authoring/checking costs and remaining dependencies.

* Measured completed cells record actual elapsed session intervals with overlapping build waits apportioned approximately. Cells explicitly marked `retained estimate, actual n/a` preserve a planning weight only; completed-scope totals are not cumulative actual time.
* Open work classes:
  * A · pinning, configuration, and mechanical curation
  * B · semantic porting and tool re-homing with differential tests
  * C · fresh systems authoring with functional tests
  * D · RTL and FPGA work
* Confidence: every open item's range spans roughly a factor of two about its midpoint, and a class believed softer is priced by widening its own items' ranges rather than by a second figure stated over the total.
* The [current summary](#current-summary) gives the total and range derived from the item cells.
* **Calibration is measured separately for each authority pool.** [The attended record](#calibration-record) and [the agent-parallel record](#the-agent-parallel-series) pair each measured item with its earliest estimate. The generated table below reports their results. Unmeasured retained estimates do not enter either fit.
* **The ratio informs range width; it does not re-price item midpoints.** The attended external-authoring pool is limited, so the calibrated total remains a separate planning view. Individual items can meet, exceed or fall below their estimates.
* **Attended and agent-parallel fits are kept separate.** Attended actuals measure elapsed attended intervals, while agent-parallel actuals measure summed agent-session wall-clock. There is no measured conversion between those clocks. The agent-parallel fit prices no item because its execution mode is not known when it is priced.
* **Strategy:** move work across the authority line and generate currently authored artifacts. The gate figures above give the remaining class-X share; accepted compiler evidence supplies M1.7's backend prerequisite.
* Horizon: the planning assumption is 10–20 attended hours per week. Apply available attended capacity to the committed-work totals and producer joins in the [current summary](#current-summary); neither gives a calendar promise or a conversion to agent-parallel time.
* M8a delivery waits for the concrete producer joins and acceptance predicates. Admit parallel work when its inputs and exclusive write boundary are settled and review/build capacity is reserved; no sum of milestone estimates predicts elapsed delivery time or fixes a lane count.

Actuals come from item cells. Missing estimates or actuals use `n/a` in both columns and are excluded; retained original estimates and ranges stay in completion notes.
